| TestREG_KERNEL_STORAGE_006_R2DeleteMapsErrors | Unit | Given the fake S3 rejects with 403/500, When Delete is called, Then the S3 error code and status are surfaced (not swallowed). | `backend/internal/kernel/storage/storage_internal_test.go` |  |
| TestREG_KERNEL_STORAGE_007_R2SizeReturnsStoredBytes | Unit | Given an object of 4321 bytes, When Size is called, Then it HEADs the key and returns the server-reported size (not a client-declared one). | `backend/internal/kernel/storage/storage_internal_test.go` |  |
| TestREG_KERNEL_STORAGE_008_R2SizeMapsErrors | Unit | Given the object is missing (404) or forbidden (403), When Size is called, Then it returns 0 and an error carrying the HTTP status. | `backend/internal/kernel/storage/storage_internal_test.go` |  |
| TestREG_KERNEL_STORAGE_009_R2NetworkFailureIsError | Unit | Given an unreachable endpoint, When Delete/Size run, Then they fail fast with an error rather than reporting success. | `backend/internal/kernel/storage/storage_internal_test.go` |  |
| TestREG_KERNEL_STORAGE_010_CloudinarySignMatchesDocumentedVector | Unit | Given Cloudinary's documented example params and secret, When sign runs, Then it yields the documented SHA-1 signature (sorted k=v&... + secret). | `backend/internal/kernel/storage/storage_internal_test.go` |  |
| TestREG_KERNEL_STORAGE_011_CloudinarySignUploadFieldsAndSignature | Unit | Given folder/public id/tags, When SignUpload runs, Then the target is a POST to the resource-type endpoint whose signature covers exactly folder,public_id,tags,timestamp (not api_key) and verifies with the secret. | `backend/internal/kernel/storage/storage_internal_test.go` |  |
| TestREG_KERNEL_STORAGE_012_CloudinarySignUploadDefaults | Unit | Given no folder/public id/type, When SignUpload runs, Then it falls back to the configured upload folder, resource type "auto", and the folder as key. | `backend/internal/kernel/storage/storage_internal_test.go` |  |
